is chosen sufficiently large).

Let us now derive from Lemma
5.2
the necessary estimates for the bootstrap argument. In the following we repeatedly use the triangular inequality to infer from (
5.10
) and (
5.11
) the corresponding control (
5.9
) with
C
depending only
C^{\rm h}
,
T^{\rm h}
(and
\kappa\leq 1
). We shall denote by
C
a constant depending uniquely on
s,k,\underline{M},M,h_{\star},h^{\star}
and
C^{\rm h}
,
T^{\rm h}
, but not on
C_{0}
, and which may change from line to line.

By means of (
5.7a
)-(
5.7b
) and Lemma
3.4
, we infer from (
5.10
)-(
5.11
)

\kappa^{1/2}\big\lVert h^{\rm d}\big\rVert_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{s,k})}+\kappa\big\lVert\nabla_{\bm{x}}h^{\rm d}\big\rVert_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{s,k})}\leq C\,\big(\big\lvert\mathcal{F}\big\rvert_{L^{1}_{T}}+\big\lvert\mathcal{F}\big\rvert_{L^{2}_{T}}\big).

Next, by differentiating with respect to space the first equation of (
5.6a
) using (
5.6b
) and Lemma
3.4
, we infer

\mu^{1/2}\kappa^{1/2}\big\lVert\nabla_{\bm{x}}\eta^{\rm d}\big\rVert_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{s,k})}+\mu^{1/2}\kappa\big\lVert\nabla_{\bm{x}}^{2}\eta^{\rm d}\big\rVert_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{s,k})}\leq C\,\big(\big\lvert\mathcal{F}\big\rvert_{L^{1}_{T}}+\big\lvert\mathcal{F}\big\rvert_{L^{2}_{T}}\big).

Now, we use (
5.6a
)-(
5.6b
)-(
5.6c
)
and proceeding as in the proof of Proposition
4.8
(together with the above estimates) we infer that for any
t\in(0,T)
,

\mathcal{F}(t)\leq C_{1}\big\lvert\mathcal{F}\big\rvert_{L^{1}_{t}}+C_{2}\big\lvert\mathcal{F}\big\rvert_{L^{2}_{t}}+C_{3}\,\mu^{1/2}M_{0}\,t

with
C_{i}
(
i\in\{1,2,3\}
) depending uniquely on
s,k,\underline{M},M,h_{\star},h^{\star}
and
C^{\rm h}
,
T^{\rm h}
.
By using the inequality
\mathcal{F}(t)\leq\mu^{1/2}M_{0}\exp(C_{0}t)
from (
5.10
) and the inequality
\tau\leq\exp(\tau)
(for
\tau\geq 0
), we deduce

\mathcal{F}(t)\leq C_{1}\mu^{1/2}M_{0}C_{0}^{-1}\exp(C_{0}t)+C_{2}\mu^{1/2}M_{0}(2C_{0})^{-1/2}\exp(C_{0}t)+C_{3}\mu^{1/2}M_{0}C_{0}^{-1}\exp(C_{0}t).

There remains to choose
C_{0}
sufficiently large so that
C_{1}C_{0}^{-1}+C_{2}(2C_{0})^{-1/2}+C_{3}C_{0}^{-1}<1
, and restrict to
\mu
sufficiently small so that
\mu^{1/2}M_{0}\exp(C_{0}T^{\rm h})\leq\mu^{1/2}M_{0}C^{1/2}/2\leq\kappa^{1/2}/2
. The upper and lower bounds for
\underline{h}+h^{\rm nh}
follow immediately from the corresponding ones for
\underline{h}+h^{\rm h}
provided by Lemma
5.1
and triangular inequality, augmenting
C
if necessary.
Then the usual continuity argument yields, as desired,
T^{\rm nh}=\min(T^{\star},T^{\rm h})
.
∎

5.4.
Improved convergence rate

Proposition
5.3
established the strong convergence for regular well-prepared initial data of the solution to the non-hydrostatic equations, (
2.1
), towards the corresponding solution to the hydrostatic equations, (
2.5
), as
\mu\searrow 0
. The convergence rate displayed in the proof is
\mathcal{O}(\mu^{1/2})
. The aim of this section is to provide an improved and
optimal
convergence rate
\mathcal{O}(\mu)
. The strategy is based on the interpretation of the non-hydrostatic solution as an approximate solution to the hydrostatic equations (in the sense of consistency) and the use of the uniform control obtained in Proposition
5.3
.

Proposition 5.4
.

There exists
p\in\mathbb{N}
such that for any
s,k\in\mathbb{N}
with
k=s>\frac{5}{2}+\frac{d}{2}
and
\underline{M},M,h_{\star},h^{\star}>0
, there exists
C=C(s,k,\underline{M},M,h_{\star},h^{\star})>0
such that under the assumptions of Proposition
5.3
and using the notations therein,

\big\lVert h^{\rm nh}-h^{\rm h}\big\rVert_{L^{\infty}(0,T^{\rm h};H^{s-1,k-1})}+\big\lVert\eta^{\rm nh}-\eta^{\rm h}\big\rVert_{L^{\infty}(0,T^{\rm h};H^{s,k})}+\big\lVert{\bm{u}}^{\rm nh}-{\bm{u}}^{\rm h}\big\rVert_{L^{\infty}(0,T^{\rm h};H^{s,k})}\leq C\,\mu.

Corollary 5.5
.

Incrementing
p\in\mathbb{N}
, we find that for any
s,k\in\mathbb{N}
such that
k=s>\frac{3}{2}+\frac{d}{2}
,

\big\lVert h^{\rm nh}-h^{\rm h}\big\rVert_{L^{\infty}(0,T^{\rm h};H^{s,k})}+\big\lVert\eta^{\rm nh}-\eta^{\rm h}\big\rVert_{L^{\infty}(0,T^{\rm h};H^{s+1,k+1})}+\big\lVert{\bm{u}}^{\rm nh}-{\bm{u}}^{\rm h}\big\rVert_{L^{\infty}(0,T^{\rm h};H^{s+1,k+1})}\leq C\,\mu

with
C=C(s+1,k+1,\underline{M},M,h_{\star},h^{\star})>0
.

Proof.

Since all arguments of the proof have been already used in slightly different contexts, we only quickly sketch the argument.

For any
p^{\prime}\in\mathbb{N}
, we may use Proposition
5.3
with indices
s+p^{\prime}
and
k+p^{\prime}
to infer the existence of the non-hydrostatic solution
(h^{\rm nh},{\bm{u}}^{\rm nh},w^{\rm nh})\in\mathcal{C}([0,T^{\rm h}];H^{s+p^{\prime},k+p^{\prime}}(\Omega)^{2+d})
and the control

\sup_{t\in[0,T^{\rm h}]}\Big(\big\lVert\eta^{\rm nh}(t,\cdot)\big\rVert_{H^{s+p^{\prime},k+p^{\prime}}}+\big\lVert{\bm{u}}^{\rm nh}(t,\cdot)\big\rVert_{H^{s+p^{\prime},k+p^{\prime}}}+\big\lvert\eta^{\rm nh}\big|_{\varrho=\rho_{0}}(t,\cdot)\big\rvert_{H^{s+p^{\prime}}_{\bm{x}}}\Big)\leq C\,M_{0}.

By using
h^{\rm nh}=-{\partial}_{\varrho}\eta^{\rm nh}
and the divergence-free condition

w^{\rm nh}=({\underline{{\bm{u}}}}+{\bm{u}}^{\rm nh})\cdot\nabla_{\bm{x}}\eta^{\rm nh}-\int_{\varrho}^{\rho_{1}}\nabla_{\bm{x}}\cdot((\underline{h}+h^{\rm nh})({\underline{{\bm{u}}}}+{\bm{u}}^{\rm nh}))\dd\varrho^{\prime}

we obtain (augmenting
C
if necessary)

\sup_{t\in[0,T^{\rm h}]}\Big(\big\lVert h^{\rm nh}(t,\cdot)\big\rVert_{H^{s+p^{\prime}-1,k+p^{\prime}-1}}+\big\lVert w^{\rm nh}(t,\cdot)\big\rVert_{H^{s+p^{\prime}-1,k+p^{\prime}-1}}\Big)\leq C\,M_{0}

and hence, by Corollary
4.2
(specifically (
4.19
)), Poincaré inequality (
4.10
) and choosing
p^{\prime}
sufficiently large, that
P_{\rm nh}(\cdot,\varrho):=P^{\rm nh}(\cdot,\varrho)-\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\varrho}\varrho^{\prime}h^{\rm nh}(\cdot,\varrho^{\prime})\dd\varrho^{\prime}
satisfies

\sup_{t\in[0,T^{\rm h}]}\big\lVert P_{\rm nh}(t,\cdot)\big\rVert_{H^{s+1,k+1}}\leq C\,\mu\,M_{0}.

From this estimate we infer (by Lemma
5.1
) that

h^{\rm d}:=h^{\rm nh}-h^{\rm h}
and
{\bm{u}}^{\rm d}:={\bm{u}}^{\rm nh}-{\bm{u}}^{\rm h}

satisfies

(5.12)

\displaystyle{\partial}_{t}\eta^{\rm d}+(\underline{{\bm{u}}}+{\bm{u}}^{\rm nh})\cdot\nabla_{\bm{x}}\eta^{\rm d}+\int_{\varrho}^{\rho_{1}}(\underline{{\bm{u}}}^{\prime}+{\partial}_{\varrho}{\bm{u}}^{\rm nh})\cdot\nabla_{\bm{x}}\eta^{\rm d}\,\dd\varrho^{\prime}+\int_{\varrho}^{\rho_{1}}(\underline{h}+h^{\rm nh})\nabla_{\bm{x}}\cdot{\bm{u}}^{\rm d}\,\dd\varrho^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\int_{\varrho}^{\rho_{1}}{\bm{u}}^{\rm d}\cdot\nabla_{\bm{x}}h^{\rm h}+h^{\rm d}\nabla_{\bm{x}}\cdot{\bm{u}}^{\rm h}\,\dd\varrho^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\kappa\Delta_{\bm{x}}\eta^{\rm d},

\displaystyle{\partial}_{t}{\bm{u}}^{\rm d}+\big((\underline{{\bm{u}}}+{\bm{u}}^{\rm nh}-\kappa\tfrac{\nabla_{\bm{x}}h^{\rm nh}}{\underline{h}+h^{\rm nh}})\cdot\nabla_{\bm{x}}\big){\bm{u}}^{\rm d}+{\frac{\rho_{0}}{\varrho}\nabla_{\bm{x}}\eta^{\rm d}|_{\varrho=\rho_{0}}}+{\frac{1}{\varrho}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\varrho}\nabla_{\bm{x}}\eta^{\rm d}\,\dd\varrho^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\big(({\bm{u}}^{\rm d}-\kappa(\tfrac{\nabla_{\bm{x}}h^{\rm nh}}{\underline{h}+h^{\rm nh}}-\tfrac{\nabla_{\bm{x}}h^{\rm h}}{\underline{h}+h^{\rm h}}))\cdot\nabla_{\bm{x}}\big){\bm{u}}^{\rm h}

\displaystyle={\bm{R}}^{\rm nh},

with
{\bm{R}}^{\rm nh}:=-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{x}}{{P_{\rm nh}}}}{\varrho}-\frac{\nabla_{\bm{x}}\eta^{\rm nh}}{\varrho(\underline{h}+h^{\rm nh})}{\partial}_{\varrho}{{P_{\rm nh}}}
satisfying
(by Lemma
A.3
and Lemma
A.6
) the bound

\sup_{t\in[0,T^{\rm h}]}\big\lVert{\bm{R}}^{\rm nh}(t,\cdot)\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\leq C\,\mu\,M_{0}.

From this, inspecting the proof of Lemma
5.2
, we infer that as long as

\mathcal{F}_{s,k}:=\big\lVert h^{\rm d}\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,k-1}}+\big\lVert\eta^{\rm d}\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}+\big\lvert\eta^{\rm d}\big|_{\varrho=\rho_{0}}\big\rvert_{H^{s}_{\bm{x}}}+\big\lVert{\bm{u}}^{\rm d}\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}+\kappa^{1/2}\big\lVert h^{\rm d}\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\leq\kappa^{1/2}M_{0},

one has for any
{\bm{\alpha}}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}
and
j\in\mathbb{N}
such that
|{\bm{\alpha}}|+j\leq s
that
\eta^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}:={\partial}_{\bm{x}}^{\bm{\alpha}}{\partial}_{\varrho}^{j}\eta^{\rm d}
,

{\bm{u}}^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}:={\partial}_{\bm{x}}^{\bm{\alpha}}{\partial}_{\varrho}^{j}{\bm{u}}^{\rm d}
and
h^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}:={\partial}_{\bm{x}}^{\bm{\alpha}}{\partial}_{\varrho}^{j}h^{\rm d}
satisfy

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\eta^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}+(\underline{{\bm{u}}}+{\bm{u}}^{\rm nh})\cdot\nabla_{\bm{x}}\eta^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}+\Big\langle\int_{\varrho}^{\rho_{1}}(\underline{{\bm{u}}}^{\prime}+{\partial}_{\varrho}{\bm{u}}^{\rm nh})\cdot\nabla_{\bm{x}}\eta^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}\,\dd\varrho^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\int_{\varrho}^{\rho_{1}}(\underline{h}+h^{\rm nh})\nabla_{\bm{x}}\cdot{\bm{u}}^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}\dd\varrho^{\prime}\Big\rangle_{j=0}-\kappa\Delta_{\bm{x}}\eta^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}

\displaystyle=R_{{\bm{\alpha}},j},

\displaystyle\partial_{t}{\bm{u}}^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}+\big(({\underline{{\bm{u}}}}+{\bm{u}}^{\rm nh}-\kappa\tfrac{\nabla_{\bm{x}}h^{\rm nh}}{\underline{h}+h^{\rm nh}})\cdot\nabla_{\bm{x}}\big){\bm{u}}^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}+\Big\langle\frac{\rho_{0}}{\varrho}\nabla_{\bm{x}}\eta^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}\big|_{\varrho=\rho_{0}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varrho}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\varrho}\nabla_{\bm{x}}\eta^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}\dd\varrho^{\prime}\Big\rangle_{j=0}

\displaystyle={\bm{R}}^{\rm nh}_{{\bm{\alpha}},j},

and

\partial_{t}h^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}+(\underline{{\bm{u}}}+{\bm{u}})\cdot\nabla_{\bm{x}}h^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}=\kappa\Delta_{\bm{x}}\partial_{\varrho}^{j}h^{({\bm{\alpha}},j)}+r_{{\bm{\alpha}},j}+\nabla_{\bm{x}}\cdot{\bm{r}}_{{\bm{\alpha}},j},

with

\big\lVert R_{{\bm{\alpha}},j}\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\big\lVert{\bm{R}}^{\rm nh}_{{\bm{\alpha}},j}\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\leq C\,\big(\mathcal{F}_{s,k}+M_{0}\kappa\big\lVert\nabla_{\bm{x}}h^{\rm d}\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\big)+C\,\mu\,M_{0}

and

\kappa^{1/2}\big\lVert r_{{\bm{\alpha}},j}\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\big\lVert{\bm{r}}_{{\bm{\alpha}},j}\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\leq C\,\mathcal{F}_{s,k}.

We may then proceed as in the proof of Proposition
3.6
, and bootstrap the control

\mathcal{F}_{s,k}(t)+\kappa^{1/2}\big\lVert\nabla_{\bm{x}}\eta^{\rm d}\big\rVert_{L^{2}(0,t;H^{s,k})}+\kappa^{1/2}\big\lvert\nabla_{\bm{x}}\eta^{\rm d}\big|_{\varrho=\rho_{0}}\big\rvert_{L^{2}(0,t;H^{s}_{\bm{x}})}+\kappa\big\lVert\nabla_{\bm{x}}h^{\rm d}\big\rVert_{L^{2}(0,t;H^{s,k})}\leq C\,\mu\,M_{0}

(choosing
C
large enough) on the time interval
[0,T^{\rm h}]
. This concludes the proof.
∎

Appendix A
Product, composition and commutator estimates

In this section we collect useful estimates in the spaces

H^{s,k}(\Omega)
introduced in (
2.8
).
Our results will follow from standard estimates in Sobolev spaces
H^{s}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
(see
e.g.

[
Lannesbook
]
*Appendix B and references therein), and the following continuous embedding. Henceforth we denote
\Omega=\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(\rho_{0},\rho_{1})
.

Lemma A.1
.

For any
s\in\mathbb{R}
and
\rho_{0}<\rho_{1}
,
H^{s+1/2,1}(\Omega)\subset\mathcal{C}^{0}([\rho_{0},\rho_{1}];H^{s}(\mathbb{R}^{d}))
and there exists
C>0
such that for any
F\in H^{s+1/2,1}(\Omega)
,

\max_{\varrho\in[\rho_{0},\rho_{1}]}\big\lvert F(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s}_{\bm{x}}}\leq C\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s+1/2,1}}.

More generally, for any
k\geq 1
,
H^{s+1/2,1}(\Omega)\subset\bigcap_{j=0}^{k-1}\mathcal{C}^{j}([\rho_{0},\rho_{1}];H^{s-j}(\mathbb{R}^{d}))
, and in particular, for any
s_{0}>d/2
and
j\in\mathbb{N}
,
H^{j+s_{0}+\frac{1}{2},j+1}(\Omega)\subset\big(\mathcal{C}^{j}(\Omega)\cap W^{j,\infty}(\Omega)\big)
.

Proof.

By a density argument, we only need to prove the inequality for smooth functions
F
. Set
\phi:[\rho_{0},\rho_{1}]\to\mathbb{R}^{+}
a smooth function such that
\phi(\rho_{0})=0
and
\phi(\varrho)=1
if
\varrho\geq\tfrac{\rho_{0}+\rho_{1}}{2}
, and deduce that for any
\varrho\geq\tfrac{\rho_{0}+\rho_{1}}{2}
, recalling the notation
\Lambda^{s}:=(\Id-\Delta_{\bm{x}})^{s/2}
,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}(\Lambda^{s}F)^{2}({\bm{x}},\varrho)\dd{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\varrho}\partial_{\varrho}\big(\phi(\varrho^{\prime})(\Lambda^{s}F)^{2}({\bm{x}},\varrho^{\prime})\big)\dd\varrho^{\prime}\dd{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\big\lvert\phi\big\rvert_{L^{\infty}_{\varrho}}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{1}}\big\lvert\Lambda^{s}F(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{1/2}_{\bm{x}}}\big\lvert\Lambda^{s}\partial_{\varrho}F(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{-1/2}_{\bm{x}}}\dd\varrho

\displaystyle\quad+\big\lvert\phi^{\prime}\big\rvert_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{1}}\big\lvert\Lambda^{s}F(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{L^{2}}\big\lvert\Lambda^{s}F(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{L^{2}}\dd\varrho

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s+1/2,0}}^{2}+\big\lVert\partial_{\varrho}F\big\rVert_{H^{s-1/2,0}}^{2}

Using symmetrical considerations when
\varrho<\tfrac{\rho_{0}+\rho_{1}}{2}
, we prove the claimed inequality, which yields the first continuous embedding. Higher-order embeddings follow immediately.
∎

Recall the notation

A_{s}+\big\langle B_{s}\big\rangle_{s>s_{\star}}=\begin{cases}A_{s}&\text{ if }s\leq s_{\star}\,,\\
A_{s}+B_{s}&\text{otherwise.}\end{cases}

Product estimates

Recall the standard product estimates in Sobolev spaces
H^{s}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
.

Lemma A.2
.

Let
d\in\mathbb{N}^{\star}
,
s_{0}>d/2
.

(1)

For any
s,s_{1},s_{2}\in\mathbb{R}
such that
s_{1}\geq s
,
s_{2}\geq s
and
s_{1}+s_{2}\geq s+s_{0}
, there exists
C>0
such that for any
f\in H^{s_{1}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and
g\in H^{s_{2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
,
fg\in H^{s}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and

\big\lvert fg\big\rvert_{H^{s}}\leq C\big\lvert f\big\rvert_{H^{s_{1}}}\big\lvert g\big\rvert_{H^{s_{2}}}.

(2)

For any
s\geq-s_{0}
, there exists
C>0
such that for any
f\in H^{s}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and
g\in H^{s}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\cap H^{s_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
,
fg\in H^{s}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and

\big\lvert fg\big\rvert_{H^{s}}\leq C\big\lvert f\big\rvert_{H^{s_{0}}}\big\lvert g\big\rvert_{H^{s}}+C\left\langle\big\lvert f\big\rvert_{H^{s}}\big\lvert g\big\rvert_{H^{s_{0}}}\right\rangle_{s>s_{0}}.

(3)

For any
s_{1},\dots,s_{n}\in\mathbb{R}
such that
s_{i}\geq 0
and
s_{1}+\dots+s_{n}\geq(n-1)s_{0}
, there exists
C>0
such that for any
(f_{1},\dots,f_{n})\in H^{s_{1}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\times\cdots\times H^{s_{n}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
,
\prod_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and

\big\lvert\prod_{i=1}^{n}f_{i}\big\rvert_{L^{2}}\leq C\prod_{i=1}^{n}\big\lvert f_{i}\big\rvert_{H^{s_{i}}}.

Let us turn to product estimates in
H^{s,k}(\Omega)
spaces.

Lemma A.3
.

Let
d\in\mathbb{N}^{\star}
,
s_{0}>d/2
. Let
s,k\in\mathbb{N}
such that
s\geq s_{0}+\frac{1}{2}
and
1\leq k\leq s
. Then
H^{s,k}(\Omega)
is a Banach algebra and there exists
C>0
such that for any
F,G\in H^{s,k}(\Omega)
,

\big\lVert FG\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\leq C\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}.

Moreover, if
s\geq s_{0}+\frac{3}{2}
and
2\leq k\leq s
, then there exists
C^{\prime}>0
such that for any
F,G\in H^{s,k}(\Omega)
,

\big\lVert FG\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\leq C^{\prime}\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,k-1}}+C^{\prime}\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,k-1}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}},

and if
s\geq s_{0}+\frac{3}{2}
and
k=1
, then there exists
C^{\prime\prime}>0
such that for any
F,G\in H^{s,k}(\Omega)
,

\big\lVert FG\big\rVert_{H^{s,1}}\leq C^{\prime\prime}\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,1}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,1}}+C^{\prime\prime}\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,1}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s,1}}.

Proof.

We set two multi-indices
{\bm{\beta}}=({\bm{\beta}}_{\bm{x}},{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho})\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
and
{\bm{\gamma}}=({\bm{\gamma}}_{\bm{x}},{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho})\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
being such that
|{\bm{\beta}}|+|{\bm{\gamma}}|\leq s
and
{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}+{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}\leq k
. Let us first assume furthermore that
{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}\leq k-1
and
|{\bm{\gamma}}|\leq s-1
. Then

\displaystyle\big\lVert(\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F)(\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G)\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{1}}\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\beta}}|}_{\bm{x}}}^{2}\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\gamma}}|-\frac{1}{2}}_{\bm{x}}}^{2}\dd\varrho

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\lVert\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F\big\rVert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\beta}}|,0}}^{2}\big\lVert\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G\big\rVert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\gamma}}|,1}}^{2}\leq\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}^{2}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}^{2}.

where we used Lemma
A.2
(
1
) with
(s,s_{1},s_{2})=(0,s-|{\bm{\beta}}|,s-|{\bm{\gamma}}|-\frac{1}{2})
, and Lemma
A.1
. If
{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}=k
or
|{\bm{\gamma}}|=s
, and since
1\leq k\leq s
, we have
{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}\leq k-1
and
|{\bm{\beta}}|\leq s-1
and we may make use of the symmetric estimate. Hence the proof of the first statement follows from Leibniz rule.

For the second statement, we assume first that
\max(\{{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho},{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}\})\leq k-1
and
\max(\{|{\bm{\beta}}|,|{\bm{\gamma}}|\})\leq s-1
. Then, using Lemma
A.2
with
(s,s_{1},s_{2})=(0,s-|{\bm{\beta}}|-\frac{1}{2},s-|{\bm{\gamma}}|-1)
(recall
s\geq s_{0}+\frac{3}{2}
), and Lemma
A.1
,

\displaystyle\big\lVert(\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F)(\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G)\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\lvert\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\beta}}|-\frac{1}{2}}_{\bm{x}}}\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\gamma}}|-1}_{\bm{x}}}\big\rvert_{L^{2}_{\varrho}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}+1}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}}}\leq\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,k-1}},

Then if
{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}=k
or
|{\bm{\beta}}|=s
, we have (since
s\geq k\geq 2
)
{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}\leq k-2
and
|{\bm{\gamma}}|\leq s-2
, and we infer

\displaystyle\big\lVert(\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F)(\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G)\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\lvert\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\beta}}|}_{\bm{x}}}\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\gamma}}|-\frac{3}{2}}_{\bm{x}}}\big\rvert_{L^{2}_{\varrho}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}+1}}\leq\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,k-1}}.

Of course we have the symmetrical result when
{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}=k
or
|{\bm{\gamma}}|=s
, which complete the proof.

Finally, for the last statement, we consider first the case
{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}=0
and
\max(\{|{\bm{\beta}}|,|{\bm{\gamma}}|\})\leq s-1
, and infer as above

\big\lVert(\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F)(\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G)\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\lesssim\big\lvert\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\beta}}|-\frac{1}{2}}_{\bm{x}}}\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\gamma}}|-1}_{\bm{x}}}\big\rvert_{L^{2}_{\varrho}}\lesssim\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,1}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,1}}.

The case
{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}=1
(and hence
{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}=0
) and
\max(\{|{\bm{\beta}}|,|{\bm{\gamma}}|\})\leq s-1
is treated symmetrically. Then if
|{\bm{\beta}}|=s
we have
{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}=|{\bm{\gamma}}|=0
, and we infer

\big\lVert(\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F)(\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G)\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\lesssim\big\lvert\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\beta}}|}_{\bm{x}}}\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\gamma}}|-\frac{3}{2}}_{\bm{x}}}\big\rvert_{L^{2}_{\varrho}}\lesssim\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,1}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,1}}.

The case
|{\bm{\gamma}}|=s
is treated symmetrically, and the proof is complete.
∎

Composition estimates

Let us recall the standard composition estimate in Sobolev spaces
H^{s}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
.

Lemma A.4
.

Let
d\in\mathbb{N}^{\star}
,
s_{0}>d/2
. For any
\varphi\in\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R};\mathbb{R})
such that
\varphi(0)=0
, and any
M>0
, there exists
C>0
such that for any
f\in H^{s_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\cap H^{s}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
with
\big\lvert f\big\rvert_{H^{s_{0}}}\leq M
, one has
\varphi(f)\in H^{s}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and

\big\lvert\varphi(f)\big\rvert_{H^{s}}\leq C\big\lvert f\big\rvert_{H^{s}}.

We now consider composition estimates in
H^{s,k}(\Omega)
.

Lemma A.5
.

Let
d\in\mathbb{N}^{\star}
,
s_{0}>d/2
. Let
s,k\in\mathbb{N}
with
s\geq s_{0}+\frac{1}{2}
and
1\leq k\leq s
, and
M>0
. There exists
C>0
such that for any
\varphi\in W^{s,\infty}(\mathbb{R};W^{k,\infty}((\rho_{0},\rho_{1})))
with
\varphi(0;\cdot)\equiv 0
, and any
F\in H^{s,k}(\Omega)
such that
\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\leq M
, then
\varphi\circ F:({\bm{x}},\varrho)\mapsto\varphi(F({\bm{x}},\varrho);\varrho)\in H^{s,k}(\Omega)
and

\big\lVert\varphi\circ F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\leq C\big\lvert\varphi\big\rvert_{W^{s,\infty}(\mathbb{R};W^{k,\infty}((\rho_{0},\rho_{1})))}\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}.

If moreover
s\geq s_{0}+\frac{3}{2}
and
2\leq k\leq s
, then there exists
C^{\prime}>0
such that for any
F\in H^{s,k}(\Omega)
such that
\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,k-1}}\leq M
,

\big\lVert\varphi\circ F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\leq C^{\prime}\big\lvert\varphi\big\rvert_{W^{s,\infty}(\mathbb{R};W^{k,\infty}((\rho_{0},\rho_{1})))}\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}.

Proof.

Let
{\bm{\alpha}}=({\bm{\alpha}}_{\bm{x}},{\bm{\alpha}}_{\varrho})\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}\setminus\{{\bf 0}\}
with
0\leq|{\bm{\alpha}}|\leq s
and
0\leq{\bm{\alpha}}_{\varrho}\leq k
. We have by Faà di Bruno’s formula

\big\lVert\partial^{\bm{\alpha}}(\varphi\circ F)\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\lesssim\sum\big\lVert\big(({\partial}_{1}^{i}{\partial}_{2}^{j}\varphi)\circ F\big)\,(\partial^{{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1}}F)\cdots(\partial^{{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{i}}F)\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)},

where
i,j\in\mathbb{N}
with
i+j\leq|{\bm{\alpha}}|\leq s
, and the multi-indices
{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell}=({\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell,{\bm{x}}},{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell,\varrho})\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}\setminus\{{\bf 0}\}
satisfy
\sum_{\ell=1}^{i}{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell,{\bm{x}}}={\bm{\alpha}}_{\bm{x}}
and
j+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i}{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell,\varrho}={\bm{\alpha}}_{\varrho}
. If
i=0
then we have from the mean value theorem that for any
0\leq j\leq k

\big\lVert({\partial}_{2}^{j}\varphi)\circ F\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}=\big\lVert({\partial}_{2}^{j}\varphi)\circ F-({\partial}_{2}^{j}\varphi)\circ 0\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\leq\big\lvert{\partial}_{1}{\partial}_{2}^{j}\varphi\big\rvert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}\times(\rho_{0},\rho_{1}))}\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}.

The case
i=1
is straightforward, and we now focus on the case
i\geq 2
. We assume without loss of generality that
|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1,\varrho}|\geq|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{2,\varrho}|\geq\cdots\geq|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{i,\varrho}|
and remark that for
\ell\neq 1
,
|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell,\varrho}|\leq k-1
(otherwise
|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1,\varrho}|+|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell,\varrho}|=2k>k\geq|{\bm{\alpha}}_{\varrho}|
) and
|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell}|\leq s-|{\bm{\alpha}}^{1,j}_{1}|\leq s-1
.
Hence we have

\displaystyle\textstyle\big\lVert\prod_{\ell=1}^{i}(\partial^{{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell}}F)\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\textstyle\lesssim\big\lvert\big\lvert\partial^{{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1}}F\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1}|}_{\bm{x}}}\big(\prod_{\ell=2}^{i}\big\lvert\partial^{{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell}}F\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{2}|-\frac{1}{2}}_{\bm{x}}}\big)\big\rvert_{L^{2}_{\varrho}}

\displaystyle\textstyle\lesssim\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1,\varrho}}}\big(\prod_{\ell=2}^{i}\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell,\varrho}+1}}\big)\leq\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}^{i}

where we used Lemma
A.2
(
3
) and
(i-1)(s-\frac{1}{2})\geq(i-1)s_{0}
and Lemma
A.1
. The first claim follows.

Now we assume additionally that
k\geq 2
and
s\geq s_{0}+\frac{3}{2}
. The cases
i\in\{0,1\}
can be treated exactly as above and we deal only with the case
i\geq 2
, ordering
|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1,\varrho}|\geq|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{2,\varrho}|\geq\cdots\geq|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{i,\varrho}|
as above. Assume first that
|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1,\varrho}|=k\geq 2
. Then for all
\ell\neq 1
,
|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell,\varrho}|=0
and
|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell}|\leq s-2
, and we conclude as before with

\textstyle\big\lVert\prod_{\ell=1}^{i}(\partial^{{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell}}F)\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\lesssim\big\lvert\big\lvert\partial^{{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1}}F\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1}|}_{\bm{x}}}\big(\prod_{\ell=1}^{i}\big\lvert\partial^{{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell}}F\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell}|-\frac{3}{2}}_{\bm{x}}}\big)\big\rvert_{L^{2}_{\varrho}}\lesssim\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,1}}^{i-1}.

Otherwise we have
|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{2,\varrho}|\leq|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1,\varrho}|\leq k-1
and
|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{2}|\leq s-|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1}|\leq s-1
and notice that for
\ell\geq 3
,
|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell,\varrho}|\leq k-2
(since otherwise we have the contradiction
|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1,\varrho}|+|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{2,\varrho}|+|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{3,\varrho}|\geq 3(k-1)\geq k+1\geq|{\bm{\alpha}}_{\varrho}|+1
) and
|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell}|\leq s-|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1}|-|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{2}|\leq s-2
. Hence

\displaystyle\textstyle\big\lVert\prod_{\ell=1}^{i}(\partial^{{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell}}F)\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\textstyle\lesssim\big\lvert\big\lvert\partial^{{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1}}F\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1}|-\frac{1}{2}}_{\bm{x}}}\big\lvert\partial^{{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{2}}F\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{2}|-1}_{\bm{x}}}\big(\prod_{\ell=3}^{i}\big\lvert\partial^{{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell}}F\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell}|-\frac{3}{2}}_{\bm{x}}}\big)\big\rvert_{L^{2}_{\varrho}}

\displaystyle\textstyle\lesssim\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{1,\varrho}+1}}\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{2,\varrho}}}\big(\prod_{\ell=3}^{i}\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,{\bm{\alpha}}^{i,j}_{\ell,\varrho}+1}}\big)\lesssim\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,k-1}}^{i-1}.

This concludes the proof.
∎

We shall apply the above to estimate quantities such as (but not restricted to)

\Phi:({\bm{x}},\varrho)\in\Omega\mapsto\frac{h({\bm{x}},\varrho)}{\underline{h}(\varrho)+h({\bm{x}},\varrho)},

with
\underline{h}\in W^{k,\infty}((\rho_{0},\rho_{1}))
and
h\in H^{s,k}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
satisfying the condition
\inf_{({\bm{x}},\varrho)\in\Omega}\underline{h}(\varrho)+h({\bm{x}},\varrho)\geq h_{\star}>0
. Let us detail the result and its proof for this specific example.

Lemma A.6
.

Let
d\in\mathbb{N}^{\star}
,
s_{0}>d/2
. Let
s,k\in\mathbb{N}
with
s\geq s_{0}+\frac{1}{2}
and
1\leq k\leq s
, and
M,\underline{M},h_{\star}>0
. There exists
C>0

such that for any
\underline{h}\in W^{k,\infty}((\rho_{0},\rho_{1}))
with
\big\lvert\underline{h}\big\rvert_{W^{k,\infty}_{\varrho}}\leq\underline{M}
and any
h\in H^{s,k}(\Omega)
with
\big\lVert h\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\leq M
and satisfying the condition
\inf_{({\bm{x}},\varrho)\in\Omega}\underline{h}(\varrho)+h({\bm{x}},\varrho)\geq h_{\star}
, then

\Phi:({\bm{x}},\varrho)\mapsto\frac{h({\bm{x}},\varrho)}{\underline{h}(\varrho)+h({\bm{x}},\varrho)}\in H^{s,k}(\Omega),

and

\big\lVert\Phi\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\leq C\big\lVert h\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}.

If moreover
s>\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2}
and
2\leq k\leq s
, then the above holds for any
h\in H^{s,k}(\Omega)
with
\big\lVert h\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,k-1}}\leq M
.

Proof.

We can write
\Phi=\varphi\circ h
with
\varphi(\cdot,\varrho)=f(\cdot,\underline{h}(\varrho))
where
f\in\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
is set such that
f(y,z)=\frac{y}{y+z}
on
\omega:=\{(y,z)\ :\ \ |y|\leq\big\lVert h\big\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)},\ |z|\leq\big\lvert\underline{h}\big\rvert_{L^{\infty}((\rho_{0},\rho_{1}))},\ y+z\geq h_{\star}\}
. We can construct
f
as above such that the control of
\big\lvert\varphi\big\rvert_{W^{s,\infty}(\mathbb{R};W^{k,\infty}((\rho_{0},\rho_{1})))}
depends only on
\big\lVert h\big\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}
(which is bounded appealing to Lemma
A.1
, if
h\in H^{s,k}
with
s>\frac{d}{2}+\frac{1}{2},\,1\leq k\leq s
),
\big\lvert\underline{h}\big\rvert_{W^{k,\infty}((\rho_{0},\rho_{1}))}
and
h_{\star}>0
.
The result is now a direct application of Lemma
A.5
.∎

Commutator estimates

We now recall standard commutator estimates in
H^{s}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
.

Lemma A.7
.

Let
d\in\mathbb{N}^{\star}
,
s_{0}>d/2
and
s\geq 0
.

(1)

For any
s_{1},s_{2}\in\mathbb{R}
such that
s_{1}\geq s
,
s_{2}\geq s-1
and
s_{1}+s_{2}\geq s+s_{0}
, there exists
C>0
such that for any
f\in H^{s_{1}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and
g\in H^{s_{2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
,
[\Lambda^{s},f]g:=\Lambda^{s}(fg)-f\Lambda^{s}g\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and

\big\lvert[\Lambda^{s},f]g\big\rvert_{L^{2}}\leq C\big\lvert f\big\rvert_{H^{s_{1}}}\big\lvert g\big\rvert_{H^{s_{2}}}.

(2)

There exists
C>0
such that for any
f\in L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
such that
\nabla f\in H^{s-1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\cap H^{s_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and for any
g\in H^{s-1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, one has
[\Lambda^{s},f]g\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and

\big\lvert[\Lambda^{s},f]g\big\rvert_{L^{2}}\leq C\big\lvert\nabla f\big\rvert_{H^{s_{0}}}\big\lvert g\big\rvert_{H^{s-1}}+C\left\langle\big\lvert\nabla f\big\rvert_{H^{s-1}}\big\lvert g\big\rvert_{H^{s_{0}}}\right\rangle_{s>s_{0}+1}.

(3)

There exists
C>0
such that for any
f,g\in H^{s}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\cap H^{s_{0}+1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, the symmetric commutator
[\Lambda^{s};f,g]:=\Lambda^{s}(fg)-f\Lambda^{s}g-g\Lambda^{s}f\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and

\big\lvert[\Lambda^{s};f,g]\big\rvert_{L^{2}}\leq C\big\lvert f\big\rvert_{H^{s_{0}+1}}\big\lvert g\big\rvert_{H^{s-1}}+C\big\lvert f\big\rvert_{H^{s-1}}\big\lvert g\big\rvert_{H^{s_{0}+1}}.

The validity of the above estimates persist when replacing the operator
\Lambda^{s}
with the operator
\partial^{\bm{\alpha}}
with
{\bm{\alpha}}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}
a multi-index such that
|{\bm{\alpha}}|\leq s
.

We conclude with commutator estimates in the spaces
H^{s,k}(\Omega)
.

Lemma A.8
.

Let
d\in\mathbb{N}^{\star}
,
s_{0}>d/2
. Let
s\geq s_{0}+\frac{3}{2}
and
k\in\mathbb{N}
such that
2\leq k\leq s
. Then there exists
C>0
such that for any
{\bm{\alpha}}=({\bm{\alpha}}_{\bm{x}},{\bm{\alpha}}_{\varrho})\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
with
|{\bm{\alpha}}|\leq s
and
{\bm{\alpha}}_{\varrho}\leq k
, one has

\big\lVert[\partial^{\bm{\alpha}},F]G\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\leq C\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,\min(\{k,s-1\})}}.

Proof.

We set two multi-indices
{\bm{\beta}}=({\bm{\beta}}_{\bm{x}},{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho})\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
and
{\bm{\gamma}}=({\bm{\gamma}}_{\bm{x}},{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho})\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
with
{\bm{\beta}}+{\bm{\gamma}}={\bm{\alpha}}
, and
|{\bm{\gamma}}|\leq s-1
. Assume first that
{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}\leq k-1
and
|{\bm{\beta}}|\leq s-1
. Then

\displaystyle\big\lVert(\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F)(\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G)\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\lvert\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\beta}}|-\frac{1}{2}}_{\bm{x}}}\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\gamma}}|-1}_{\bm{x}}}\big\rvert_{L^{2}_{\varrho}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}+1}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}}}\leq\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,\min(\{k,s-1\})}},

where we used Lemma
A.2
(
1
) with
(s,s_{1},s_{2})=(0,s-|{\bm{\beta}}|-\frac{1}{2},s-|{\bm{\gamma}}|-1)
, and Lemma
A.1
. Otherwise
{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}=0
and
|{\bm{\gamma}}|\leq s-|{\bm{\beta}}|\leq s-2
, and we have

\displaystyle\big\lVert(\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F)(\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G)\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\lvert\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\beta}}|}_{\bm{x}}}\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\gamma}}|-\frac{3}{2}}_{\bm{x}}}\big\rvert_{L^{2}_{\varrho}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,1}}\leq\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s,k}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,\min(\{k,s-1\})}}.

The claim follows from decomposing
[\partial^{\bm{\alpha}},F]G
as a sum of products as above.
∎

Lemma A.9
.

Let
d\in\mathbb{N}^{\star}
,
s_{0}>d/2
. Let
s\geq s_{0}+\frac{5}{2}
and
k\in\mathbb{N}
such that
2\leq k\leq s
. Then there exists
C>0
such that for any
{\bm{\alpha}}=({\bm{\alpha}}_{\bm{x}},{\bm{\alpha}}_{\varrho})\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
with
|{\bm{\alpha}}|\leq s
and
{\bm{\alpha}}_{\varrho}\leq k
, one has

\big\lVert[\partial^{\bm{\alpha}};F,G]\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\leq C\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,\min(\{k,s-1\})}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,\min(\{k,s-1\})}}.

Proof.

We can decompose

[\partial^{\bm{\alpha}};F,G]=\sum_{{\bm{\beta}}+{\bm{\gamma}}={\bm{\alpha}}}(\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F)(\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G)

with multi-indices
{\bm{\beta}}=({\bm{\beta}}_{\bm{x}},{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho})\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
and
{\bm{\gamma}}=({\bm{\gamma}}_{\bm{x}},{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho})\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}
such that
|{\bm{\beta}}|+|{\bm{\gamma}}|\leq s
and
{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}+{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}\leq k
, and
1\leq|{\bm{\beta}}|,|{\bm{\gamma}}|\leq s-1
. Assume furthermore that
{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}\leq k-1
and
|{\bm{\beta}}|\leq s-2
. Then

\displaystyle\big\lVert(\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F)(\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G)\big\rVert_{L^{2}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\lvert\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\beta}}F(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\beta}}|-\frac{3}{2}}_{\bm{x}}}\big\lvert\partial^{\bm{\gamma}}G(\cdot,\varrho)\big\rvert_{H^{s-|{\bm{\gamma}}|-1}_{\bm{x}}}\big\rvert_{L^{2}_{\varrho}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}+1}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}}}\leq\big\lVert F\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,\min(\{k,s-1\})}}\big\lVert G\big\rVert_{H^{s-1,\min(\{k,s-1\})}},

where we used Lemma
A.2
(
1
) with
(s,s_{1},s_{2})=(0,s-|{\bm{\beta}}|-\frac{3}{2},s-|{\bm{\gamma}}|-1)
, and Lemma
A.1
.
By symmetry, the result holds if
{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}\leq k-1
and
|{\bm{\gamma}}|\leq s-2
. Hence there remains to consider the situation where (
{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}=k
or
|{\bm{\beta}}|=s-1
) and (
{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}=k
or
|{\bm{\gamma}}|=s-1
). Since
s>2
and
|{\bm{\beta}}|+|{\bm{\gamma}}|\leq s
, we cannot have
|{\bm{\beta}}|=|{\bm{\gamma}}|=s-1
. In the same way, we cannot have
{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}={\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}=k
since
k>0
. Furthermore , we cannot have
{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}=k
and
|{\bm{\gamma}}|=s-1
, since the former implies
|{\bm{\beta}}|\geq{\bm{\beta}}_{\varrho}=k\geq 2
and the latter implies
|{\bm{\beta}}|\leq 1
. Symmetrically, we cannot have
{\bm{\gamma}}_{\varrho}=k
and
|{\bm{\beta}}|=s-1
. This concludes the proof.
∎
